I couldn't take screenshots because the Browser pane was hidden, so I checked the page text instead. **Light and dark mode haven't been looked at.**

**Files created** (all in `apps/web/src`, none marked "use client"):
- `components/app/container.tsx`: `Container`, a div with the page width (`--container`), gutters, `py-6` and `gap-6`. It takes div props, and `className` is merged.
- `components/app/page-header.tsx`: `AppPageHeader({ title, description?, actions?, children? })` wraps `ui/page-header`. `children` sits under the title row, for `PageHeaderStats`.
- `components/app/empty-state.tsx`:
  - `EmptyState({ icon?, title, description?, action?, reason?: "empty"|"no-results", variant?: "framed"|"plain" })`, with the icon drawn as a Phosphor duotone glyph.
  - Presets:
    - `NoSessionsEmpty`, which links to /setup with the primary button.
    - `NoCardsDueEmpty`, with no action.
    - `NoReviewsEmpty`, which links to /review with the secondary button.
    - `NoResultsEmpty({ query, noun="sessions", action? })`, which writes the query back in the title and defaults to `plain`.
- `components/app/error-state.tsx`:
  - `ErrorState({ title, message?, statusWord="Failed", status?: "fail"|"warn", code?, action?, children? (support/details), announce?, variant? })`.
  - It sets `role="alert"` only when `announce` is true; otherwise `role="none"`, because `Empty` adds alert by default.
  - Presets:
    - `SessionFailedState({ error })`: shows the stored `sessions.error`, with a generic line when it is null.
    - `LoadFailedState({ noun, code?, action? })`.
    - `NotFoundState({ noun="Session", action? })`: "Not found" in the warn colour, with a default link back to /dashboard.
- `components/app/loading-state.tsx`:
  - `SessionListLoading({ rows=4, max 8 })`, `DecisionCardLoading`, `StatRowLoading` and `ReviewCardLoading`. Each has one `<output class="sr-only">` that says what is loading; the skeleton blocks are hidden from screen readers.
  - `ProcessingState({ status: "pending"|"processing" })` shows the StatusBadge, a title and an indeterminate `Progress value={null}`.
- `components/app/status-badge.tsx`: `StatusBadge({ status, live? })` on `ui/status`. pending is "Queued", processing is "Making cards" (with the turning arc), ready is "Ready", failed is "Failed".
- `components/app/score-badge.tsx`: `ScoreBadge({ score: 0|1|2 })` and the `Score` type. 2 is "Explained" (success), 1 is "Partly" (warning), 0 is "Missed" (default).
- `components/app/stat-row.tsx`: `StatRow({ cardsDueToday?, sessionsThisWeek?, explainedPercent?: number|null, loading? })`, one framed strip of 3 stats. A null percent shows "None" with "No reviews yet".
- `components/app/session-row.tsx`: `SessionRow({ session, cardCount?, now: Date, live? })`.
  - Title falls back to "repo on branch", then "Untitled session"; the repo shows in mono under a real title.
  - Card count shows only when the session is ready; a failed row shows its error with the fail wash.
  - The time is relative, inside `<time dateTime>`. Only ready rows link to `/s/[id]`.
- `lib/relative-time.ts` and `lib/relative-time.test.ts` (both new): `formatRelativeTime(iso, now)` with a fixed en-US locale. The caller passes the clock, so nothing reads `Date.now` during render.
- `components/design-system/catalog-app.tsx` (new): `AppCatalog` uses a fixed `NOW` and shows every status, score, empty, error, loading and pending state.
- `app/design-system/page.tsx`: I added only the import, a SECTIONS entry `{ id: "app", label: "App blocks" }` and `<AppCatalog />` after `<BlocksGallery />`.

**Skipped:** `decision-card-preview.tsx`. The plan gives `DecisionCard` to Phase 5 (Kasam), with props to be agreed with Zia, so a generic preview now would get ahead of that.

**Needed from `ui/` but not done:**
1. `ItemLink` is a plain `<a>` with no `render` prop, so a session row can't use `next/link` and loses client-side navigation and prefetch. `<Item render={<Link/>}>` would replace the row's `listitem` role, because `ItemGroup` is `role="list"`. I marked it with a `shortcut:` comment.
2. `ui/skeleton.tsx` has no `SkeletonText` or `SkeletonSwap`. My loaders use fixed `h-4`/`h-5` boxes and the caller switches on its own loading flag. The `ui` Skeleton also has `animate-pulse`, which Quartz's skeleton rule bans.
3. `ui/empty.tsx` has no `EmptyRetry`, so `ErrorState` takes a retry `action` slot instead of a promise-aware retry.
4. `nav-sessions.tsx` has its own copy of the status map with different labels ("Pending", "Processing"). It could use `StatusBadge`, but I didn't edit it.

**TODO(zia):**
- `score-badge.tsx`: the score words "Explained", "Partly" and "Missed". The docs give only the numbers.
- `stat-row.tsx`: what "Explained back" counts — answers scored 2, or ARCHITECTURE.md's "can still explain".
- `empty-state.tsx`: which fields search reads, for the no-results line.
- `error-state.tsx`: the next step for a learner when the pipeline stored no error (a re-upload returns 409).
- `loading-state.tsx`: a time estimate in `ProcessingState`.
- The gallery has placeholder copy: the header action, a failed-session error text, a request id and a custom empty title.

My own choices: the status labels "Queued" and "Making cards", and showing not found in the warn colour (it's a 4xx).

I fixed 10 of the 14 comments and partly fixed 1. I left 3 alone because the fix belongs in other files or would mean a redesign. tsc shows no errors in my files. oxlint shows 0 errors (5 warnings in code-block.tsx and item.tsx, none on lines I changed). oxfmt has been run. I added `src/components/ui/chart-scale.test.ts`, and it passes 2/2. I changed no git state.

- 4412686894 | fixed | `onCopy` now runs after the try/catch, and only when the copy worked. A callback that throws is no longer shown as a failed copy.
- 4412686934 | fixed | The observer now watches the sticky anchor instead of the title, with a 1px-deeper rootMargin. The bar only shows once the anchor has reached its sticky edge, so it can no longer appear over the stats or content. It now appears a little later than before: after the whole header, not just the title, has scrolled away. I fixed it this way because moving the anchor in front of the header would add a flex gap above the header, which changes the layout. The header comment now says "header" instead of "title". I also dropped a second `+ top`: `rootBounds` already has the rootMargin applied.
- 4412686978 | fixed | `markRefs` now skips refs whose `text` is empty, so `indexOf("")` can't loop forever.
- 4412687001 | not-fixed | `item.tsx` is not a client file and is imported by server pages, so `Item` can't use context to tell whether it is inside an `ItemGroup`. A blanket `role="listitem"` would also be wrong for standalone rows and for button or link rows. The app pages already pass `role="listitem"`. The callers listed below need the same.
- 4412687028 | fixed | Added `condenseTop` to the observer effect's dependencies.
- 4412687068 | partly | The doc example in `chart.tsx` now uses `var(--hue-3-line)`, which is emitted. The claim checks out: `--color-chart-*` are only declared in `@theme inline reference` in `globals.css`, so the variable doesn't exist at runtime. The callers or `globals.css` still need a change (listed below).
- 4412687076 | fixed | Rewrote the awaited-confirm example. It holds `onOpenChange` while pending, disables Cancel and Action, and catches the rejection to show an error. It no longer leaves an unhandled rejection. Building the pending gate into `AlertDialog` itself is out of scope for this PR.
- 4412687084 | fixed | The alert popup now has `max-h-[calc(100%-var(--gutter-sm)*2)] overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain`, so a tall alert scrolls instead of running off the screen. This matches the width calc.
- 4412687122 | fixed | `AvatarBadge` now has rules for `xs` (`size-2`) and `2xs` (`size-1.5`), with the svg hidden on both.
- 4412687132 | fixed | The addon's disabled colour now uses `group-has-[[data-slot=input-group-control]:disabled]/input-group:`, the same condition the group already uses. Nothing ever set `data-disabled`.
- 4412687156 | fixed | Ticks are now computed by index from a count capped at 1000, so `niceScale(1e16, 1e16 + 4)` ends. A test covers it.
- 4412687237 | fixed | `isEmpty` now counts an array as empty when every element is empty, so an empty `.map()` shows the 'None' fallback.
- 4412687350 | fixed | `tokenize` is wrapped in `useMemo` keyed on source, language, refs and detectRefs, so toggling wrap doesn't tokenize again. There is no React Compiler in this app.
- 4412687356 | not-fixed | The actions are arbitrary ReactNode. Stripping ids from the bar's copy would mean deep-cloning the tree or a new API, such as separate actions for the bar. That is a redesign for later. The current caller in `app/design-system/page.tsx` puts no ids in its actions.

**Files outside my set that need a change:**
- **Chart colours (4412687068):** either emit `--color-chart-1`…`5` at runtime in `src/app/globals.css` (a plain `@theme` or `:root` block), or point these at `var(--hue-*-line)`:
  - `src/components/progress/answers-chart.tsx:13`
  - `src/components/design-system/catalog-data.tsx:30-31`
- **Item rows that need `role="listitem"` (4412687001):**
  - `src/components/design-system/catalog-display.tsx:339`: the `<div className="contents">` wrapper is the group's child, so the role goes on it.
  - `src/components/design-system/blocks/session-blocks.tsx:103` and `:166`
  - `src/components/design-system/blocks/dialog-patterns-block.tsx:181`
  - `src/components/design-system/blocks/dialog-patterns-block.tsx:126`: these are button rows. Wrap each in a listitem element, or drop the list role on that group.
  - `src/components/app/loading-state.tsx:32` sits inside an `aria-hidden` group, so it doesn't matter.

I haven't checked the condensed bar or the alert-dialog scroll in a browser.

Fixed 15 of the 16 comments in my 11 owned files; 1 is not-fixed (the reviewer's claim is wrong). No file outside my set needs a change.

Checks, run from apps/web on my 11 files: oxfmt done; oxlint 0 errors (4 warnings, none in lines I touched); `tsc --noEmit -p .` reports no errors in my files. Nothing is checked in a browser.

Choice on the spinners: under reduced motion they now turn slowly (3s per turn) instead of stopping, so the loading cue stays. This differs from the reviewer's `motion-safe:animate-spin` and from CopyButton and Status's running arc, which stop. Normal motion looks the same as before.

4412686807 | fixed | The slider now treats a number `value` or `defaultValue` as one thumb; `[min, max]` is used only when neither is passed.
4412686874 | not-fixed | `countId` is referenced directly by `aria-describedby`, and the accessible-name spec includes hidden nodes that are referenced directly, so browsers still read the count as the description. The visual count stays hidden by itself.
4412686924 | fixed | Removed the `sidebar_state` cookie write and its two constants. Nothing read the cookie, and reading it would make the app layout dynamic.
4412686938 | fixed | Bar sparklines now include 0 in the upper bound too, so all-negative bars hang from the top inside the viewBox.
4412686950 | fixed | Without `thumbLabels`, range thumbs are now named from the root's `aria-label` plus a number ("Price 1", "Price 2"). The prop comment now says to pass `thumbLabels` for ranges. A range with no `aria-label` and no `thumbLabels` is still unnamed.
4412687017 | fixed | `SidebarMenuSubButton` now closes the mobile sheet when it is a link, using the same anchor-only handler as `SidebarMenuButton`.
4412687166 | fixed | A queued span with no `end` now ends at its start, so it draws no bar and has no elapsed time. Its duration label shows zero.
4412687185 | fixed | Added `motion-reduce:animate-none` to the Skeleton pulse.
4412687217 | fixed | Fixed the comment, not the behavior: it now says there is no default max height, and that passing one (for example `max-h-40`) makes the text scroll with the edge fade. I did not add a default max height, because that would change how existing textareas look.
4412687228 | fixed | `StatDelta` now returns null when only one of `from` and `to` is passed and there are no `children`, instead of rendering an empty "No change".
4412687262 | fixed | The live region now announces `word` itself, so screen readers hear the same custom content that sighted users see.
4412687312 | fixed | Replaced `aria-label` on the `<output>` with a visually hidden "Loading" text inside it.
4412687367 | fixed | The toast's loading icon still spins, but turns slowly under reduced motion (`motion-reduce:[animation-duration:3s]`) instead of stopping.
4412687398 | fixed | The Spinner turns slowly under reduced motion instead of stopping, so the loading state stays visible. The file comment says so.
4412687409 | fixed | Gated both toast pulse classes with `motion-safe:`. The pulse is unchanged when reduced motion is off.
4412687419 | fixed | `SidebarMenuSubButton` now sets `aria-current="page"` when `isActive`, like `SidebarMenuButton`.
4412687429 | fixed | Removed the `no-scrollbar` class: nothing defines it, so removing it changes nothing visible. If the sidebar should hide its scrollbar, the utility has to be added in `styles/utilities.css`, which is a separate decision.

Edited files (all in /Users/ziaurrehmankhan/code/personal/session-coach/.claude/worktrees/design-system-landing-shell-57b973/apps/web/src/components/ui/): sidebar.tsx, skeleton.tsx, slider.tsx, sparkline.tsx, spinner.tsx, stat.tsx, status.tsx, textarea.tsx, timeline.tsx, toast.tsx. segmented-control.tsx is unchanged.

**Files** (all under `apps/web/src`)
- `app/not-found.tsx`: catches every unknown URL. It has the marketing nav and footer, a small "Error 404" label and a large display heading, "This page isn't *in the deck.*" Below that are one plain line and two buttons, "Go home" and "Open the dashboard". A last line links to support ("Followed a link from Session Coach? Tell support"). The ruled index-card band from the landing hero sits at the bottom of the page. Title is "Page not found | Session Coach".
- `app/(app)/not-found.tsx`: the (app) layout does wrap it, so the sidebar stays. It shows the not-found error state with the heading "We couldn't find that", one line on why, and "Back to the dashboard". It only appears when an (app) page calls `notFound()` and has no closer not-found page of its own; unknown URLs go to the root page. `/s/[id]` already has its own "Session not found" page, which still wins there.
- `components/errors/server-error-state.tsx` (client, shared): built on `ErrorState`.
  - It logs the error with `console.error`.
  - It shows the heading "Something went wrong on our side", one plain line, and the error `digest` as "Ref …" when there is one. It never shows the error message or stack.
  - It has "Try again" (calls `retry()`), a way out, and a "Contact support" link.
  - Next 16 passes the boundary `{ error: unknown, retry }`. `reset` still exists but the docs prefer `retry`; I used `retry`.
- `components/errors/error-page.tsx` (client): a header with just the brand linking home, around `ServerErrorState`.
- `app/error.tsx`: uses `ErrorPage`, with "Go home" as the way out.
- `app/(app)/error.tsx`: `ServerErrorState` inside `Container` with "Back to the dashboard", so the sidebar stays.
- `app/global-error.tsx`: renders its own `<html>` and `<body>`, imports `./globals.css`, adds a `<title>`, and runs the theme script so light/dark matches the app. It does not load the brand fonts, so it uses the system font.
- `app/(marketing)/login/page.tsx`:

  - Heading "Sign *in*" in the display font, one line, then `LoginForm`.
- `components/auth/login-form.tsx`:
  - "Continue with Google", an "or" divider, then labelled email and password fields with "Forgot password?" next to the password label, and a "Create an account" link.
  - The plan puts sign-up on its own page (`/sign-up`), so this is a link, not a toggle.
  - Validation: email required and must look like an email; password required. The 10-character minimum applies only at sign-up, per the plan.
  - Errors are tied to their fields with `aria-describedby` and `aria-invalid`, and focus moves to the first invalid field.
  - On a valid submit, or a Google click, a notice appears: "Sign-in isn't open yet. Accounts arrive with the next release. Nothing you typed was sent or saved." It is announced to screen readers.
  - The form has no action and calls `preventDefault`, and Google is a plain button. There are no network calls.

**Checks:** `pnpm format`, `typecheck`, `lint`, `test` and `pnpm --filter web build` all pass. Lint has no errors; the 19 warnings are all in files that were already there. Tests: 7 and 11 pass, 0 fail. The safe-next tests cover `/x`, `//evil.com`, `/\evil.com`, `https://evil.com`, `javascript:` and empty. `check:secrets` is clean.

**Curl against `next start --port 3071`** (server stopped afterwards):
- Signed out, `/dashboard` → 307 to `/login?next=%2Fdashboard`. `/s/abc?x=1`, `/review` and `/settings` redirect the same way.
- 200: `/login`, `/sign-up`, `/auth/error?code=x`, `/auth/confirm`, `/update-password`, `/`.
- The 404 page returns 404.
- `/auth/error?code=link_expired` shows its copy; an unknown or script-like code gets the generic copy.
- Signed out, `/` and the 404 page show only "Sign in": no sample user and no email in the HTML.
- `/login?next=/review` puts `/review` into the form's hidden `next` field.
- `/auth/confirm?token_hash=…&type=email` shows the "Confirm and sign in" button.

**Files** (all under `apps/web/src`):
- **Proxy:** `proxy.ts` and `lib/supabase/proxy.ts`.
  - The proxy calls `getClaims()` first and copies the cookies and cache headers that `setAll` hands it onto the response, redirects included.
  - Signed-out visits to `/dashboard`, `/s`, `/review`, `/progress`, `/settings` and `/setup` go to `/login?next=…`. Signed-in visits to `/login` or `/sign-up` go to `safeNext(next)`, which is `/dashboard` by default.
  - The matcher skips `_next/static`, `_next/image`, `api/dashboard`, `favicon.ico` and files with an extension.
- **Auth helpers in `lib/auth/`:**
  - `safe-next.ts` and its test.
  - `profile.ts`: `getProfile()` is wrapped in React `cache`, uses the RLS client, selects the six columns by name, and parses with the shared `profileSchema` minus `created_at`.
  - `signup-email.ts`: the name of the cookie that remembers the sign-up address.
  - `current-user.ts`: `getCurrentUser` is now wrapped in `cache`; its signature is unchanged.
- **Server Actions** in `app/(auth)/actions.ts`: `signIn`, `signUp`, `signInWithGoogle`, `requestReset`, `updatePassword`, `resendConfirmation`, `signOut` (scope local) and `signOutEverywhere` (scope global).
  - Error codes map to friendly copy: `invalid_credentials` gets one generic message, `email_not_confirmed` brings up the resend button, rate limits get "wait a minute", and weak passwords show their reasons.
  - Sign-up always lands on check-email, including for `user_already_exists`.
  - `signOut` takes no arguments, so `<form action={signOut}>` works anywhere.
- **Pages:**
  - `(auth)/login` reads `?next` inside Suspense.
  - `(auth)/sign-up/check-email` has a resend button with a 60 s cooldown. The address comes from an httpOnly cookie scoped to `/sign-up`, never from the URL.
  - New: `(auth)/update-password/page.tsx`, `auth/confirm/page.tsx` with its action in `auth/confirm/actions.ts`, `auth/callback/route.ts`, `auth/error/page.tsx`, and `auth/layout.tsx`, which reuses the (auth) layout.
- **Components:**
  - The login, sign-up and forgot-password forms now use `useActionState`. Their markup and a11y patterns are kept. There is a new `update-password-form.tsx`.
  - New shared pieces: `auth-notice.tsx`, `google-form.tsx` and `resend-confirmation.tsx`.
- **Sign-out:** in `nav-user.tsx` and `marketing/site-nav-account.tsx`, the menu item submits a hidden form placed outside the menu popup, so it is a POST. `nav-user.tsx` ended up in the final wiring after your correction, and its props stay non-null.
- **Header:** `site-nav.tsx` no longer uses `sampleUser`. It reads the real user from `getProfile`, falling back to the email claim, inside `<Suspense>` with a Skeleton fallback. Nothing user-related is cached. `not-found.tsx` needed no change.
- **`.env.example`:** one comment line saying the anon key is the chosen browser key.

**Choices you should know about:**
- **Origin in Server Actions:** the redirect URLs are built from the `Origin` header. Next.js only runs an action when Origin matches the host, which is its CSRF check. Route handlers use `request.nextUrl.origin`. The reasoning is written in a comment in `actions.ts`; it belongs in `docs/DECISIONS.md`, which I don't own.
- **Confirm-link actions:** `confirmEmail` accepts the types signup, invite, magiclink, recovery, email_change and email. Otherwise it sends the learner to `/auth/error` with a code.

**Not done or not verified:**
- **Per-IP rate limit (#22):** not built. It needs a shared store; an in-memory limit doesn't hold on serverless.
- **Real flows:** never run end to end. I created no accounts, as instructed, so sign-up, confirm, Google, reset and sign-out are untested against the live project, and nothing was checked in a browser.
- **Profiles migration:** `getProfile` depends on `20261029000000_auth_profiles.sql` being applied live. If it isn't, the header logs the error and shows the email.

**Changes needed in files I don't own:**
1. `src/app/(app)/layout.tsx` still passes `sampleUser` to AppSidebar. You said you changed AppSidebar to accept null; the layout should now pass the real user from `getProfile` or `getCurrentUser`, inside Suspense.
2. Settings needs a "Sign out everywhere" button: `<form action={signOutEverywhere}>`, imported from `@/app/(auth)/actions`.
3. `docs/DECISIONS.md` needs an entry for the Origin-header choice.
4. The Supabase email templates must use `{{ .RedirectTo }}&token_hash={{ .TokenHash }}&type=email` for confirm signup and `type=recovery` for reset. This is a manual dashboard step.

   ```ts
   // A round icon key for a control-height avatar (size-8): hover, press and focus ring follow the circle.
   "icon-round": "size-(--control) rounded-full! px-0",
   ```
2. `apps/web/src/components/marketing/site-nav-account.tsx`, the trigger: `render={<Button variant="ghost" size="icon" />}` becomes `render={<Button variant="ghost" size="icon-round" />}`. It stays ghost.
3. Same file, the avatar: `<Avatar size="sm" aria-hidden>` becomes `<Avatar aria-hidden>`. The default size is `size-8`, 32px, the same as `--control` and the Dashboard button. I put this comment above it: `{/* Default size (32px, --control): the circle is as tall as the Dashboard button beside it. */}`.

Lane B is done. Commit `ecba63f` on branch `worktree-agent-a57797ceb2ab081f4`, after a fast-forward to `zia/design-system` (87bf7b4). Format, typecheck, lint (0 errors) and tests all pass, and `pnpm --filter web build` succeeds with /dashboard as partial prerender. I haven't seen it work with real data or Realtime, because no signed-in learner was available.

**Files** (all under `apps/web/`)
- `src/lib/sessions/rows.ts`: pure helpers (column list, `PAGE_SIZE=20`, `parsePage`, `localDate(tz, now)`, `applySessionChange`, `toSummary`) and the types `SessionSummary` / `SessionListItem` (summary plus optional `cardCount`).
- `src/lib/sessions/rows.test.ts`: 4 tests (page parsing, timezone date across midnight, update in place, prepend and limit).
- `src/lib/sessions/queries.ts` (server-only, RLS client, columns by name, never `model_answer`):
  - `listSessions({ userId, page })` returns `{ sessions, hasMore }`. It fetches 21 rows to know whether an older page exists.
  - `recentSessions(userId, limit = 5)`.
  - `dueTodayCount(userId, now)` uses `profiles.timezone`, then counts reviews with `due_on <= local today` and `mastered = false`.
  - Card counts come from selecting `decision_cards.session_id` for ready sessions and counting in JS.
- `src/lib/sessions/use-live-sessions.ts`: client hook. It subscribes to `postgres_changes` on `sessions` with `user_id=eq.<uid>`, updates rows in place, prepends new ones, and removes the channel on unmount. When a session becomes ready it fetches the card count in the browser. It raises a toast when a session settles on ready or failed; a shared Set stops the dashboard and the sidebar toasting the same change twice.
- `src/components/sessions/session-list.tsx`: client list. Shows "No sessions yet" + /setup when there are no rows.
- `src/app/(app)/dashboard/page.tsx`: Suspense with the existing `SessionListLoading`, the due-today banner linking to /review, and Newer/Older page links. A signed-out visitor gets the "No sessions yet" empty state.
- `src/app/(app)/dashboard/error.tsx`: "Couldn't load your sessions" with a Try again button.
- `src/components/nav-sessions.tsx`, the redesigned sidebar row:
  - Line 1 is the title, truncated.
  - Line 2 is a folder icon and the repo, then a small Badge: "Queued" (outline), "Making cards" (default), "Cards ready" (success) or "Failed" (fail).
  - Screen readers hear "Repository:" and "Status:" before each part, and hovering shows a native tooltip with the full title, repo and status.
  - The group is hidden when there are no sessions.
  - No badge or status variant was added.

**Departures from the brief**
- **No nuqs:** it isn't installed and I couldn't add dependencies, so the page number is read from `searchParams` directly. This is marked with a `shortcut:` comment.
- **No `src/components/sessions/session-row.tsx`:** I reused the existing `src/components/app/session-row.tsx`, which already does everything asked.
- **StatRow removed:** the old sample stats strip is gone because its numbers were fake.
- **Queries take `userId`:** the caller checks the user first (authorization rule); the brief had `listSessions({ page })`.
- **Due-today query location:** the plan's maintenance note wants it in `src/lib/review/` for the daily email cron. It's in `queries.ts` as you asked; move it when Phase 6 lands.

**Wiring you need to add** (I didn't edit these files):
1. `src/components/app-sidebar.tsx`: change the prop to `sessions: SessionListItem[]` (from `@/lib/sessions/rows`), add `userId?: string | null`, and render `<NavSessions sessions={sessions} userId={userId} />`.
2. `src/app/(app)/layout.tsx`: drop the fixture and load inside Suspense (cacheComponents), roughly:
   ```tsx
   const user = await getCurrentUser();
   const sessions = user ? await recentSessions(user.id) : [];
   <AppSidebar sessions={sessions} userId={user?.id ?? null} user={...} />
   ```
   The sidebar only goes live once `userId` is passed; without it the rows just show as rendered.

**Not verified**
- Realtime insert and update on an open dashboard, the toasts, the RLS card counts, and the due-today count against real rows. There was no signed-in learner, and I wrote nothing to the database.
- A smoke run of `next start` showed the signed-out /dashboard (and `?page=abc`) rendering "No sessions yet" with no errors in the log.
- No light/dark screenshots were taken.

**Known limits**
- The hook keeps its rows in local state and ignores new server props. The dashboard list resets on page change (keyed by page), but the sidebar won't pick up a `router.refresh()`.
- DELETE events are ignored.

It calls `DELETE /api/account`; on success it shows a toast and goes to `/`, on failure it toasts the server's message.
- `lib/settings/profile.ts`: `getSettingsProfile()` reads the four columns by name. `timeZoneOptions(current)` returns UTC, the saved zone and every IANA name, sorted. It runs on the server, so server and browser can't render different lists.

- `app/api/account/route.ts`, in order:
  1. `getUser()`; 401 if nobody is signed in.
  2. With the admin client, collect every `sessions.raw_path` for the learner, plus everything listed under `<user_id>/` in the `sessions` bucket (catches files with no session row).
  3. Delete those objects in batches of 1000.
  4. `auth.admin.deleteUser(id)`, which cascades.
  5. `signOut({ scope: "local" })`. auth-js clears the cookies even though the user is already gone.
  - Any failure returns 500 `{ error }` and is logged with the route name. A failure before step 4 leaves the account usable, so the learner can retry.
- `app/(marketing)/delete-account/page.tsx`: a public, static page. It points signed-in learners to Settings and tells others to email `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPPORT_EMAIL || "[email redacted]"`.
- `app/sitemap.ts`: adds `/delete-account`.

NEVER read, cat, grep or print any .env file other than .env.example, and never print key values. Run scripts with `node --env-file=apps/web/.env.local …`.

Build `scripts/seed-demo.mjs` (plain Node ESM, using @supabase/supabase-js already in apps/web — resolve it from there, no new dependencies) with service-role access, and root package.json scripts `seed:demo` and `seed:demo:live`:
- `--email <address>` (required): finds the existing auth user by email via auth.admin.listUsers paging. NEVER create auth users or set passwords. If not found, exit with a clear message telling the person to sign up first.
- Preflight (read-only): check the migration 20261029000000_auth_profiles is applied (profiles.display_name selectable). If not, stop and say so; do NOT run `supabase db push`.
- Inserts for that user: ~12 sessions over the last 3 weeks across 3–4 repos (e.g. session-coach, rapporteur, billing-api) with realistic titles, branches, durations and statuses (mostly ready, one processing, one pending, one failed with a friendly error); decision cards for ready sessions (stated and inferred, quotes in evidence that validate, so most are visible; include one card whose evidence makes it flagged per the generated-column rule); reviews at day 1/3/7 with some due today and some mastered; answers with scores 0/1/2. Respect every constraint; any Storage paths may be left null if allowed.
- Every inserted row is tagged so it can be removed (use a deterministic UUID namespace or a marker the schema allows, e.g. a title prefix is NOT acceptable if it shows in UI — prefer deterministic ids recorded in a manifest file under scripts/.seed-demo/<user-id>.json, gitignored via .git/info/exclude is NOT shared, so add the path to the repo .gitignore).
- `--reset`: deletes exactly the rows in the manifest (children first), nothing else.
- `--simulate`: picks the demo pending session and flips it pending → processing → ready over ~20 s (and one to failed), printing each step, to test the Realtime badge.
- `--dry-run`: prints counts only.
- Idempotent: re-running replaces this user's demo rows instead of duplicating.

Run ONLY `--dry-run` and the preflight against the live project (no writes) — the lead will run the real seed with the user's email after review. Print no emails other than the one passed, no keys.

The demo seed script is written and committed. The `20261029000000_auth_profiles` migration is applied on the live project. The real seed has not run: I only ran the read-only preflight and dry-run against live, so no rows were written.

**Branch:** worktree branch `worktree-agent-a92b4599b2f74f61f`, commit 57697f4 `feat(db): seed-demo script for one learner's live demo data`. That branch did not contain 87bf7b4, so I reset the clean branch to 87bf7b4 before starting.

**Files**
- `/Users/ziaurrehmankhan/code/personal/session-coach/.claude/worktrees/agent-a92b4599b2f74f61f/scripts/seed-demo.mjs`: plain Node ESM. It loads `@supabase/supabase-js` from the copy `apps/web` already installs, so there is no new dependency.
- `package.json` (root): `seed:demo` runs the dry run (it passes `--dry-run`). `seed:demo:live` does the real writes. Both use `node --env-file=apps/web/.env.local`.
- `.gitignore`: adds `scripts/.seed-demo/`, where the manifests go (`scripts/.seed-demo/<user-id>.json`).

**How it behaves**
- **Finding the user:** `--email` looks up the existing auth user through paged `auth.admin.listUsers`. It never creates users or sets passwords. If the address is unknown it exits 1 with "Sign up in the app with that address first". I checked this with `[email redacted]`.
- **Ids:** every row gets a fixed UUID v5 built from the user id and a key, and the manifest records them all. A re-run deletes the manifest's ids plus the current ids, children first and always filtered by `user_id`, then inserts again. So re-running replaces the demo rows instead of duplicating them.
- **`--reset`:** deletes only the manifest's rows (answers, then reviews, cards, sessions), then deletes the manifest. If there is no manifest it says so and deletes nothing.
- **`--simulate`:** the pending `session-coach/kasam/cli-hook` session goes to processing after 4 s. The processing `billing-api/feat/usage-metering` session goes to failed (with a friendly error) after another 8 s. The cli-hook session goes to ready after another 8 s, and its 2 cards are inserted at that moment. Each step is printed. It refuses to run if the seed hasn't run. Running the seed again puts both sessions back.
- **Schedule:** answers replay each card's score history on the day 1/3/7 rules. A due date that falls in the past is moved to today. "Due today" is worked out in the learner's `profiles.timezone`.
- **Generated columns:** `flagged` and `hidden` are never written. An assert-based check runs before any write, covering evidence shape, mastered matching an empty due date, step range and duplicate ids.

**Preflight + dry-run output (live, read-only)**
```
preflight       ok: 20261029000000_auth_profiles is applied (profiles.display_name exists)
sessions        12 (ready 9, processing 1, pending 1, failed 1) across 3 repos
decision_cards  26 (stated 17, inferred 9; flagged 1, hidden 1) + 2 added by --simulate
answers         73 (score 2: 64, 1: 6, 0: 3)
reviews         22 (mastered 9, due today 7, due later 6)
never answered  3 visible cards (in the queue if from the last 7 days)
dry run         nothing written
```
The 3 repos are session-coach, rapporteur and billing-api.

**Where the schema couldn't take what was asked**
- **`sessions.raw_path`** is `NOT NULL` and can't be empty, so it can't be left null. Demo sessions point to `<user-id>/<external-id>.jsonl.gz` and no file exists there, so anything that downloads the raw log for a demo session will get a not-found error.
- **One hidden card:** the PDF-export session has a card whose evidence is all unverified, so learners never see it.

**Checks:** `pnpm format` and `pnpm format:check` are clean. `pnpm lint` exits 0; its warnings are already in `apps/web` and none come from the script. `oxlint scripts/seed-demo.mjs` is clean, and so is `node --check scripts/seed-demo.mjs`.

**Commands for the lead** (from the worktree root, after linking `apps/web/.env.local` as in setup):
```
pnpm seed:demo --email <their-email>          # dry run with user lookup
pnpm seed:demo:live --email <their-email>     # real seed
pnpm seed:demo:live --email <their-email> --simulate
pnpm seed:demo:live --email <their-email> --reset
```
Run reset from the same checkout that ran the seed, because the manifest is gitignored and local to it.

Not checked: the real insert path and the simulate flow, since neither has run against live. The answer and review rows written mid-history have no live check either.

  - Score 2 moves step 0→1 (+1 day), 1→2 (+3), 2→3 (+7). Passing step 3 sets mastered with `due_on` null.
  - Score 0 or 1 sets step 1, due tomorrow.

  - Ownership and `hidden` are checked in code (404). Then the model is called, with one more call if the output is invalid (502 after that).

- **Old sample helpers:** `sampleQueue`, `getQuizCard`, `quizCardIds` and `sampleGrade` in `lib/sample/cards.ts` are no longer used; I left them in place as asked.
- **Answering ahead of schedule:** any visible card can be answered at any time, so four quick correct answers in a row master a card. The plan doesn't say to block this; it needs a product call.
- **No rate limit:** the plan doesn't ask for one.
- **Not atomic:** the answer insert and the reviews upsert are two writes. Two submits at once could advance a card twice, and if the upsert fails the card stays due.
- **Untested against real models:** responses wrapped in code fences would fail parsing and end in a 502. The plan's STOP rule (5 manual tries with the real model) is still open.
- **Hosted environments:** `MODEL_PROVIDER=fake` is refused under `next build`/`start` and on Vercel previews, because they run with `NODE_ENV=production`.

1. **Auth form logic (worth it).** The same validate-then-Server-Action logic is copied in 4 forms: `useActionState`, `clientErrors`, `handleSubmit` and focus. Sign in" footer (7 copies), the live region (4) and the card shell (4) repeat too. Fix: move the column into `(auth)/layout.tsx` and add small children-only parts (`AuthHeading`, `AuthCard`, `AuthStatus`, `AuthSwitch`) plus one `BrandLink` (3 copies). 3. **Field wiring (conditional).** Every form field hand-writes its ids, `aria-invalid` and `aria-describedby`. A `FormField` that hands those to the input through a render prop fits this case honestly. If the primitives pass switches to Base UI `Field`, this wrapper becomes throwaway, so do it only if that pass is more than about 2 weeks away.
4. **Session naming (worth it).** The rule for naming a session is copied 3 times, and the toast uses a different version. `SessionRow`'s `live` prop is never passed by any caller and should be deleted.
5. **One Realtime subscription (later).** The sidebar and the dashboard each open their own channel, and a module-level Set stops double toasts. A provider in the app layout should own the single channel. Do it with the `/s/[id]` Realtime TODO.
6. **Marketing cards (worth it, needs a pixel diff).** The icon-title-body card is mapped 6 times, so add a `FeatureCard`. Section headings are written three different ways; merging them is a design call for you, not a refactor.
7. **Settings cards (small).** Add a `SettingsCard` with the action as children, or give `CardTitle` a heading `render` in the primitives pass. Pick one.
8. **Not worth changing:** the sign-out menu item rewrite, the empty/error/loading state components (they already follow the named-variant pattern) and `AppPageHeader`.

Other findings:
- **Initials mismatch.** The app layout and the site header compute initials two different ways, so the same learner can show different initials in each.
- **Primitives backlog.** `<CardTitle><h2>` is written about 15 times, and `<Item role="listitem">` needs an eslint-disable 4 times. `ItemLink` can't take `next/link`. These belong in `components/ui`.
- **React 19 rules are already met.** App code has no `forwardRef`, no `useContext` and no `cloneElement`.

The 141 unresolved threads split as VALID 102, FIXED 18, DECLINE 12, DUPLICATE 9.

**Important VALID items (no Blocking):**
1. `PRRT_kwDOVap2s86xtDYx`, styles/base.css:143. The unlayered global focus outline beats the layered `focus-visible:outline-none`, so focused inputs, textareas and selects show a ring on top of their halo. Fix: add `[class*="focus-visible:outline-none"]` to the rule's `:not()` list.
2. `PRRT_kwDOVap2s86xtl_o`, content/legal/privacy.md:37. It promises that secrets are removed as a guarantee. The same overclaim is in how-it-works/page.tsx:66 and the support FAQ. Fix: use the hedged "we try to remove" wording in all three.
3. `PRRT_kwDOVap2s86xs7kI`, session-row.tsx:40. `ItemLink` is a plain `<a>`, so session and review rows do a full page reload. The shortcut comment is still there. Fix: give `ItemLink` a Base UI `render` prop and pass `next/link`.
4. `PRRT_kwDOVap2s86xs8fT` (support/page.tsx:17), with `PRRT_kwDOVap2s86xs-Y2` (delete-account) as its duplicate. Both fall back to `[email redacted]`. For signed-out users that address is the only way to delete an account. Fix: fail the production build when the variable is unset or is the example value.
5. `PRRT_kwDOVap2s86xs8hX`, quiz-form.tsx:61. After scoring, the form is replaced and keyboard focus drops to `<body>`. Fix: focus the result heading.
6. `PRRT_kwDOVap2s86xs-XS`, forgot-password-form.tsx:83 and `requestReset` in actions.ts. The rate-limit branch shows a different notice, which reveals whether an account exists (breaks auth.md #4). `resendConfirmation` has the same branch. Fix: return `"sent"` on rate limit.
7. `PRRT_kwDOVap2s86xs-YN`, (app)/layout.tsx. `Promise.all` over the sidebar reads has no catch, so one failed read replaces the whole app shell. This thread is marked outdated but still applies. Fix: catch each read and fall back.
8. `PRRT_kwDOVap2s86xs-YY`, auth/confirm. A double-click spends the one-time token twice and sends a signed-in learner to "link expired". Fix: a `useFormStatus` submit button that disables while pending.
9. `PRRT_kwDOVap2s86xs-Yv`, delete-account/page.tsx:55. A `TODO(zia)` shows on a public page.
10. `PRRT_kwDOVap2s86xs-Y7`, (auth)/actions.ts. The per-IP rate limit that auth.md #22 requires is not implemented (no `x-forwarded-for` anywhere in src).

**Notable verdicts where I overruled a bot:**
- DECLINE:
  - `data-horizontal:` and `data-vertical:` work. They are custom variants in shadcn/tailwind.css that compile to `[data-orientation=…]`.
  - Badge `[a]:` compiles to `:is(a)`, so it already matches.
  - `esbuild: false` is fine. CI on this branch passed with it.
  - The lint glob is correct. `pnpm lint` shows no `react/refs` error.
  - The answers chart is never empty. It always gets 30 days of data.
- One partial: field.tsx:166. The bot's reasoning is wrong, but the bug is real: `group-has-` matches only descendants, so `text-balance` never applies.
- Much of the PR6 ui/ list is real but latent (no current caller hits it). I graded those Advisory.

**Gaps:** I did not check anything visually. That includes the double focus ring, the narrow site-nav Dashboard button and the mobile sidebar width; those come from reading the code and built CSS only. Severity is my own judgment.

Each entry has `threadId`, `commentDatabaseId`, `outcome`, `commit` and `reply`; the two "needs" entries also carry a `needs` array.

## Important items
- **Double focus ring (base.css):** anything with `focus-visible:outline-none` (field and select halos) is now excluded from the global outline rule (e56ea3e).
- **ItemLink:** it now takes a Base UI-style `render` prop (default `<a>`). `session-row.tsx` passes `render={<Link href=… />}` and the shortcut comment is gone (7103b3d). No other ItemLink caller exists under `components/app`.
- **field.tsx:** now `group-data-horizontal/field:text-balance` (08358d1).

## Needs (outside my paths)
- **4416470023 (ItemLink):** `apps/web/src/app/(app)/review/page.tsx:73` still renders a plain `<ItemLink href>`. It should pass `render={<Link href={`/review/${card.id}`} />}`.
- **4416521985 (Spinner):** Spinner now takes a `label` prop; `label=""` renders only the hidden icon. These callers should pass `label=""` and set `aria-busy` on the button:
  - `apps/web/src/components/setup/token-section.tsx` (lines 236 and 280)
  - `apps/web/src/components/setup/refresh-button.tsx` (line 19)

## Fixed differently from the triage suggestion
- **dialog / sheet `modal={false}`:** I removed the `modal` prop instead of adding a modal-aware overlay. No caller passes it, and `components.md` says to delete unused props.
- **Unused helpers:** I deleted `withDefaults`, `countUnitFor` and `formatCounts`, and trimmed `useOverlayOpen`'s `run`/`pending` model, instead of patching them. None had a caller.
- **copy-button and textarea:** the behaviour was intended, so only the comment changed. A forced "fetching" state ignores presses like a real fetch. The focused textarea drops its fade because a mask would also clip the focus halo.
- **ComboboxTrigger:** the "Show options" default label applies only when the trigger is icon-only, so a trigger that shows a value keeps the value as its name.
- **ComboboxItem:** the wrapper becomes a flex row only when it holds two or more parts, so plain-text items still truncate with an ellipsis.

## Screenshot comparison
Before and after builds were served with `next start` on :3082 and shot at 1280 and 375 widths. Screenshots and scripts are in `…/scratchpad/ui-fix/` (`before/`, `after/`, `regions.py`, `shift.py`, `crop.py`).

- **`/design-system` (both widths):** every changed region is one of these:

**Checks, run from the repo root:**
- `pnpm format`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint` and `pnpm test` all pass (50 web tests + 7 others).
- `pnpm --filter web build` passes.
- With `VERCEL_ENV=production` and both public vars blank, `next build` fails with the intended message.
- I did not run server changes against local Supabase, and did not check UI changes in a browser.

**Important items:**
- **Secrets wording:** `content/legal/privacy.md`, `/how-it-works` and the `/support` FAQ now all say "we try to remove secrets"… and "removal is not a guarantee" (1d8c2c0).
- **Support email and site URL:** new `src/lib/production-env.ts` (tested), checked in `next.config.ts`. A Vercel production build fails when:
  - `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL` is unset, or
  - `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPPORT_EMAIL` is unset or an `@example.com` address.

  Previews and local builds keep the fallbacks, now in `src/lib/site.ts`. `.env.example` blanks the value and documents the check (6a62bc2). The visible TODO on `/delete-account` is now a code comment.
- **Quiz form:** the result heading takes focus after scoring, using the existing `useFocusWhenReplaced`. The textarea is read-only while scoring (5ef8d27).
- **Enumeration (auth.md #4):** a rate-limited `requestReset` or `resendConfirmation` now logs and returns "sent", like a success. This also starts the resend countdown (c220321). The same commit also:
  - makes the actions use the shared `lib/auth/validate.ts` checks;
  - keeps the sign-up name after a server error;
  - hides the old notice while a submit is pending;
  - rewords the origin comment to credit the redirect allow-list.
- **`(app)/layout.tsx`:** each sidebar read catches its own error, logs `(app)/layout: <read>` and falls back to `[]`, `null` or `0` (b3e0371).
- **`/auth/confirm`:** a new `ConfirmButton` uses `useFormStatus` and is disabled while the form posts. `isOtpType` moved to `lib/auth/otp.ts` with a test. The action now checks Origin before it spends the token (84595fd).

**Needs a decision: per-IP rate limit (thread 4416488570, auth.md #22).** I added no dependency, migration or in-memory counter. An in-memory counter would not be shared across Vercel instances. The options:
1. Upstash Redis through the Vercel Marketplace: a fixed-window counter keyed on the first `x-forwarded-for` hop, checked at the top of `signIn`, `signUp` and `requestReset`. This adds a dependency. I recommend it for launch.
2. A Postgres table plus an RPC. This needs a new migration and team agreement.
3. Rely on Supabase's built-in limits and turn on Turnstile CAPTCHA (#29). This needs no code here.

**Risks to check before merging:**
- The production build guard will fail the next production deploy unless `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPPORT_EMAIL` (and `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL`) are set in Vercel production. I did not check whether they are.
- `useLiveSessions` now calls `router.refresh()` when Realtime rejoins after a dropped connection. A change in the short gap before the first join is still missed; a code comment marks that as a known limit.
- The header Dashboard button is hidden on phones, but I didn't check the 375px layout.
- Landing-page hero copy in `landing-sections.tsx` and `pricing` still says secrets are removed, without the hedge. Both files are in my area but weren't in the triage, so I left them.
- The 0-score seed change and local-noon change in `scripts/seed-demo.mjs` passed a syntax check only; I did not run the seed.
